So coordinate minimization:
```
def coord_minimize(a, n, c, deadline, sweeps=...):
  m=n//2; h=build_full(a,n)
  best_c = c.max()
  for sweep in range(...):
    # maybe random order
    for i in order:
      if time>... break
      p=i; q=n-1-i
      g = derivative_anti(h, p, q, n) # scaled? derivative of c values (already scaled) wrt x.
      L=2*n-1
      quad = np.zeros(L)
      d=q-p
      quad[n-1-d? wait index lag -d = n-1 + (-d)? Previous order index = n-1 + k. In our c order length L centered n-1. For lag +d: idx = n-1+d. For lag -d: idx = n-1-d. But above converted c previous order has center index n-1, not C order. Yes previous order index for lag k is n-1+k. So quad[n-1+d] = 1; quad[n-1-d] = 1. But need only if within [0,L-1]. d= n-1-2i, between 1 and n-1.
      # minimize max(c + g*t + quad*t^2) over t in [lo=-x, hi=1-x]
      t_opt = univariate_convex_min(c,g,quad,lo,hi)
      if t_opt close 0: continue
      a[i]=x+t_opt
      h[p]=x+t_opt; h[q]=1-(x+t_opt)
      c += g*t_opt + quad*t_opt*t_opt
      if c.max() < best_c: best_c=...
```

Function derivative for single position p (d=+1) in previous order:
```
def deriv_one(h, p, n):
  g=zeros(L)
  g[n-1] = 1 - 2*h[p]
  # negative side: idx=n-1-r for r=1..n-1
  # if p-r >=0: as first add 1-h[p-r]
  # if p+r < n: as second add -h[p+r]
  # We can loop r 1..p and 1..n-1-p.
  for r in 1..p:
    g[n-1-r] += 1 - h[p-r]
    g[n-1+r] += -h[p-r]  # positive side second? Wait positive side with k=r: if p as second when p-r>=0: contribution -h[p-r] at idx n-1+r. Yes combine.
  for r in 1..n-1-p:
    g[n-1-r] += -h[p+r]  # negative side second
    g[n-1+r] += 1 - h[p+r] # positive side first
  return g
```

`deriv_one(h,p,n)`:
```
L=2*n-1
g=np.zeros(L)
g[n-1]=1-2*h[p]
if p>0:
   r=np.arange(1,p+1)
   idx_neg=n-1-r
   idx_pos=n-1+r
   val_left=h[p-r]
   g[idx_neg]+=1-val_left
   g[idx_pos]-=val_left
if n-1-p>0:
   r=np.arange(1,n-p) # length n-1-p
   idx_neg=n-1-r
   idx_pos=n-1+r
   val_right=h[p+r]
   g[idx_neg]-=val_right
   g[idx_pos]+=1-val_right
return g
```
Check indices: For r up to n-1-p, p+r <= n-1, idx_pos up to n-1+n-1-p=2n-2-p <= L-1 okay; idx_neg from n-p to n-? for p? okay.

Coordinate sweep function:
```
def coordinate_descent_anti(a, n, c, deadline, order=None, sweeps=...):
  m=n//2
  h=build_full(a,n)
  best_cmax=c.max()* (2.0/n)
  # maybe initialize cg? no
  for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
    if time>deadline: break
    order = rng permutation m if random
    for i in order:
      x=a[i]
      # if x near bound maybe derivative check
      g=deriv_anti(h,i,n)
      d=n-1-2*i
      i1=n-1-d; i2=n-1+d
      # optional: if special indices same? no
      # minimize
      t = golden_min(c,g,i1,i2,-x,1-x)
      if abs(t)>1e-14:
         a[i]=x+t
         h[i]=x+t; h[n-1-i]=1-(x+t)
         c += g*t
         c[i1] += t*t
         c[i2] += t*t
    # recompute c to correct
    if time>... break
    h=build_full(a,n)
    c=fft_corr_count(h)
    cm=c.max()
    if cm*dx < best: best...
  return a,c,best
```

```
def golden_eval(c,g,i1,i2,t):
    v = np.max(c + g*t)
    v1 = c[i1] + g[i1]*t + t*t
    if v1>v: v=v1
    v2=...
    return v
```

Golden section details: For convex minimization on [lo,hi], standard golden with two evals. Start c1=lo+gr*(hi-lo)? Use iterative:
```
gr=(sqrt(5)-1)/2=0.618
c=lo+gr*(hi-lo); d=lo+(1-gr)*(hi-lo)
fc=F(c); fd=F(d)
for _ in range(iters):
   if fc < fd: hi=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=lo+gr*(hi-lo); fc=F(c)
   else: lo=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=lo+(1-gr)*(hi-lo); fd=F(d)
return (lo+hi)/2
```

3. Determine bracket: find grid positions around best_t. We can use `ts` sorted? We know step. Let left = best_t - step/2, right = best_t + step/2, clipped. Top-level helper:
```
def coord_f_val(c, g, buf, center, low, high, t):
    buf[:] = g
    buf *= t
    buf += c
    t2 = t*t
    buf[center] = c[center] + g[center]*t - 2.0*t2
    buf[low] = c[low] + g[low]*t + t2
    buf[high] = c[high] + g[high]*t + t2
    return float(buf.max())
```
But if low==high? d positive so distinct. If low or high equals center? Could d=0? no. If n small n=2, low=0, high=2, center=1 distinct. Good.

Local golden: We can call `coord_f_val` 2 per iteration. b:
```
def golden_min_interval(c,g,buf,center,low,high,lo,hi,iters):
   if hi-lo<1e-10: return 0.5*(lo+hi)
   gr=0.618...
   x1=lo+gr*(hi-lo); x2=hi-gr*(hi-lo)
   f1=coord_f_val(...x1); f2=...
   for ...:
      if f1 < f2: hi=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=lo+gr*(hi-lo); f1=coord_f_val(...)
      else: lo=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=hi-gr*(hi-lo); f2=coord_f_val(...)
   return (lo+hi)/2
```
Since local bracket small, okay.

```
def find_best_coord_t(c,g,buf,center,low,high,lo,hi,grid_count=17, local_iters=18):
    cur = c.max()
    best_t=0.0; best_val=cur
    if hi<=lo: return 0.0,best_val
    # grid: j=0..grid_count-1
    ts=[]? To avoid allocation, loop j:
    step=(hi-lo)/max(1,grid_count-1)
    # evaluate j=0..grid_count-1
    for j in range(grid_count): t=lo+j*step; if t < lo-... or >hi: continue; val=coord_f_val; update
    # evaluate 0 if not close to grid? Loop through all grid maybe 0 included if exact; but add eval at 0 if lo<0<hi and not near any? We already set cur=best and t=0; no need eval.
    if best_val >= cur - 1e-12: return 0.0,cur
    # determine bracket neighbors
    # find grid index closest to best_t
    jbest = int((best_t - lo) / step)
    jbest = min(max(jbest,0), grid_count-1)
    left = lo + max(0,jbest-1)*step
    right = lo + min(grid_count-1,jbest+1)*step
    # if left/right too small? use mid
    if right-left < 1e-6: local_t=best_t
    else: local_t = golden_min_interval(... left,right, local_iters)
    val = coord_f_val(... local_t)
    if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_t=local_t
    # optional try a few random perturbations? no.
    if best_val >= cur - 1e-12: return 0.0,cur
    return best_t,best_val
```

```
def start_pair_random(m, rng, density_one_per_pair=0.5):
    a=np.zeros(m)
    for i in range(m//2): # pairs (i, m-1-i) non-self if i < m-1-i => i < (m-1)/2 => i <= m//2 -1
        if rng.rand()<0.5: a[i]=1
        else: a[m-1-i]=1
    if m%2==1:
        a[m//2]=0.0 # to zero central
    return a
```

```
def upsample_first_half(a_old, m_new):
   if len==m_new: return a_old.copy()
   x_old=(np.arange(len)+0.5)/len  # [0,1]
   x_new=(np.arange(m_new)+0.5)/m_new
   return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a_old)
```

Let's implement `project_feasible` for final general:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
  target=n/2.0
  for _ in range(100): diff=target-h.sum(); if abs(diff)<1e-15 break; if diff>0: room=1-h; s=room.sum(); if s<1e-15 break; h += diff/s*room
  else: s=h.sum(); if s<1e-15 break; h += diff/s*h
  return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
Actually adding diff/s*h reduces? For diff negative, h += diff/s*h reduces proportionally. Good. But for anti, this may break anti tiny. Use final only.

```
def fft_corr_count(h, n):
    N=2*n-1
    A=np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    B=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h, N)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(B), N)
    c=np.empty(N, dtype=float)
    if n==1? We use even >=2. For n=2, n-1=1.
    c[:n-1] = C[n-1:0:-1]
    c[n-1] = C[0]
    c[n:] = C[n:N]
    # remove tiny imaginary? irfft real
    return c
```
For n=2: N=3, c[:1]=C[1:0:-1] -> C1; c[1]=C0; c[2:]=C[2:3] -> C2. Good.
For n=4: N=7, c[:3]=C[3:0:-1]: indices 3,2,1; c3=C0; c4:C[4:7]. Good.
Maybe `C` can have tiny negative counts for non-overlap? Lag0 sum h(1-h)≥0; other lags positive; due FFT roundoff maybe negative ~1e-12. Max unaffected. Could `C` include very small imaginary? irfft returns real.

Code:
```
def deriv_one_into(out, h, p, n, r_all, idx_neg, idx_pos):
    out.fill(0.0)
    center = n-1
    out[center] = 1.0 - 2.0*h[p]
    left = p
    if left > 0:
        r = r_all[:left]
        arr = h[p - r]
        idxn = idx_neg[:left]
        idxp = idx_pos[:left]
        out[idxn] += 1.0 - arr
        out[idxp] -= arr
    right = n - 1 - p
    if right > 0:
        r = r_all[:right]
        arr = h[p + r]
        idxn = idx_neg[:right]
        idxp = idx_pos[:right]
        out[idxn] -= arr
        out[idxp] += 1.0 - arr
```
Potential issue: Fancy indexing `out[idxn] += ...` when idxn is a view of precomputed array and idxn may contain repeated? No. But NumPy `out[idxn] += arr` does `out.__setitem__` with advanced index; if idxn has no duplicates, okay. But if `idxn` and `idxp` arrays overlap? Could an index appear in both negative and positive for same r? idx_neg=n-1-r, idx_pos=n-1+r distinct for r>0. Across left/right contributions for same p, could index overlap? Left negative idx for r range 1..p: indices n-1-p..n-2. Right negative for r 1..n-1-p: indices 0..n-p-2. They may overlap? Example n=10,p=4: left neg indices 5..6? wait n-1=9; left r1,2,3,4 =>8,7,6,5. right r1..5=>8,7,6,5,4. Overlap at 5,6,7,8! Indeed same lag can have both p as first and p as second? For a given r satisfying both (r≤min(p,n-1-p)), p appears both as first and second in same lag? Is that possible? Pair (p-r,p) and pair (p,p+r) for same lag -r? Negative lag -r pairs start i=0..n-1-r: h_{i+r}(1-h_i). For p first: i=p-r. For p second: i=p. Both within range if r≤p and p+r<n. Yes two different pairs in same lag involving p: (p-r,p) where p is first, and (p,p+r) where p is second. So derivative contributions add. `find_best_coord_t`: Need pass `cur_max`. It uses `buf`. Let's write:
```
def find_best_coord_t(c, g, buf, center, low, high, lo, hi, cur_max, grid_count, local_iters):
    best_t=0.0
    best_val=cur_max
    if hi <= lo + 1e-15: return 0.0, cur_max
    step=(hi-lo)/(grid_count-1)
    # to avoid evaluating 0 twice, okay
    for j in range(grid_count):
        t=lo + j*step
        if t < lo - 1e-12 or t > hi + 1e-12: continue
        if abs(t) < 1e-14:
            val=cur_max
        else:
            val=coord_f_val(c,g,buf,center,low,high,t)
        if val < best_val - 1e-14:
            best_val=val; best_t=t
    # eval at 0 cur best already, but if best_val > cur due? no.
    if best_val >= cur_max - 1e-12:
        return 0.0, cur_max
    # bracket
    j = int(((best_t - lo) / step) + 0.5)
    if j < 0: j=0
    if j > grid_count-1: j=grid_count-1
    left = lo + max(0,j-1)*step
    right = lo + min(grid_count-1,j+1)*step
    # expand if best_t near boundaries? j already.
    if right-left < 1e-8:
        local_t=best_t
    else:
        local_t=golden_min_interval(c,g,buf,center,low,high,left,right,local_iters)
    val=coord_f_val(...)
    if val < best_val:
        best_val=val; best_t=local_t
    if best_val >= cur_max - 1e-13:
        return 0.0, cur_max
    return best_t,best_val
```

```
def optimize_resolution(n, phase_end, parent_values, prev_a, seed):
  m=n//2
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+n)
  starts=[]
  if parent_values is not None: ...
  if prev_a is not None: ...
  # add deterministic starts
  for a in starts: if len==0 skip; a=clip; starts.append(a)
  # rank
  scored=[]
  for a in starts:
     c5=scoring_c(n,a)
     scored.append((c5,a))
  scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0]) # lambda not allowed? Hmm. Can use `scored.sort()` since tuples first c5. That's fine.
  # choose top k
  t_remaining = phase_end - time.time()
  if len(scored)==0: return None,None,None
  # if only one or time small: k=1
  k=1
  if t_remaining > 60 and len(scored)>=2: k=2
  if t_remaining > 150 and len(scored)>=3 and n<=2000? maybe k=3.
  fracs = {1:[1.0], 2:[0.65,0.35], 3:[0.55,0.3,0.15]}
  best_a=scored[0][1].copy(); best_c=scored[0][0]
  # precompute
  pre=make_pre(n)
  grid_counts_full = choose(n); grid_counts_micro = choose_micro(n)
  # For each top:
  for idx in range(k):
     alloc = fracs[k][idx]*t_remaining
     if alloc < 10: continue/ break? If small, skip? We still should optimize top1 with full time.
     sub_deadline = time.time()+alloc
     a0=scored[idx][1]
     # full coordinate
     a_full,c_full,h_full=coordinate_descent_anti(a0,n,sub_deadline,seed+idx,pre,...)
     # if time left, micro passes
     if time.time() < sub_deadline - 5:
        a_m1,c_m1,_, maybe = coordinate_descent_anti(a_full,n,sub_deadline,seed+100+idx,pre,max_move=0.1, ...)
        a_m2... max_move=0.02
   update best within phase if c < best_c
  return best_a,best_c,build_full(best_a,n)
```

- while time<deadline:
   if m==0 break.
   recalc? c current.
   order = rng.permutation(m)
   cur_sweep_start = cur
   significant=0
   for i in order:
      if time > deadline: break
      x=a[i]
      if x <= 0.0 or x >= 1.0:
         # still can move from bound if beneficial; lo/hi one-sided. No skip.
      p=i; q=n-1-p; d=q-p; low=center-d; high=center+d
      x = float(a[i])
      lo = -x
      hi = 1.0 - x
      if max_move is not None:
          if lo < -max_move: lo = -max_move
          if hi > max_move: hi = max_move
      if hi <= lo + 1e-14: continue
      deriv_one_into(gp,h,p,n,pre)
      deriv_one_into(gq,h,q,n,pre)
      gp -= gq
      t, val = find_best_coord_t(c,gp,fbuf,center,low,high,lo,hi,cur,g1,g2)
      if val < cur - 1e-11 and abs(t) > 1e-14:
          x_new = x + t
          if x_new < 0: x_new=0; t=x_new-x
          elif x_new > 1: x_new=1; t=x_new-x
          if abs(t) > 1e-14:
              # optional recompute val if clipped? negligible
              a[i]=x_new; h[i]=x_new; h[q]=1.0-x_new
              c += gp * t
              t2=t*t
              c[center] -= 2.0*t2
              c[low] += t2
              c[high] += t2
              cur = val
              if abs(t) > 1e-6: significant +=1
   sweeps +=1
   # recompute every sweep or if cur changed significantly
   if sweeps % 1 ==0: # every sweep
       h=build_full_anti(a,n)
       c=fft_corr_count(h,n)
       cur=c.max()
   if cur < best_c - 1e-9:
       best_c=cur; best_a=a.copy(); stalled=0
   else:
       stalled +=1
       if stalled >= 2 and significant < max(1, int(0.005*m)):
           break
   if time.time() > deadline: break
- Ensure final best_c corresponds to best_a? We recompute? Return best_a, `build_full`, `fft_corr_count`, c5.

`find_best_coord_t` details with two grid:

```
def find_best_coord_t(c,g,buf,center,low,high,lo,hi,cur_max,g1,g2):
    best_t=0.0; best_val=cur_max
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14: return 0.0,cur_max
    step=(hi-lo)/(g1-1)
    # Coarse grid
    for j in range(g1):
       t=lo + j*step
       if abs(t)<1e-14: val=cur_max
       else: val=coord_f_val(...)
       if val < best_val - 1e-14: best...
    if best_val >= cur_max - 1e-11: return 0.0,cur_max
    # bracket
    jbest=int(round((best_t-lo)/step))
    jbest=max(0,min(g1-1,jbest))
    left=lo + max(0,jbest-1)*step
    right=lo + min(g1-1,jbest+1)*step
    # If best_t at 0 and 0 not near grid? We included 0? We did not explicitly eval 0; best might be 0 if no improvement. If improvement, not 0. But if t=0 grid point not evaluated? We set val=cur for t near0; best_t remains 0 no improvement. Good.
    # fine grid
    if right > left + 1e-14:
       step2=(right-left)/(g2-1)
       for j in range(g2):
          t=left + j*step2
          if abs(t)<1e-14: val=cur_max
          else: val=coord_f_val(...)
          if val < best_val - 1e-14: best...
    if best_val >= cur_max - 1e-12: return 0.0,cur_max
    return best_t,best_val
```
Potential: If true min is at t=0 but best_val due grid lower? no.
If coarse grid best at endpoint and jbest endpoint, bracket uses neighbor; fine grid interior and endpoint, okay.

```
def interpolate_values(vals,n):
   n0=len(vals)
   if n0==n: return vals.astype(float).copy()
   x0=(np.arange(n0)+0.5)*(2.0/n0)
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
   return np.interp(x,x0,vals)
```

```
def upsample_first_half(a,m):
   old=len(a)
   if old==m: return a.astype(float).copy()
   x0=(np.arange(old)+0.5)/old
   x=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   return np.interp(x,x0,a)
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(1000):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def compute_c5_exact(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx)


def fft_corr_count(h, n):
    """Return counts in the same lag order as np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full')."""
    N = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    B = np.fft.rfft(1.0 - h, N)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A * np.conj(B), N)

    c = np.empty(N, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1
    c[:center] = C[center:0:-1]
    c[center] = C[0]
    c[center + 1:] = C[N - 1:center:-1]
    return c


def c5_from_counts(c, n):
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def build_full_anti(a, n):
    m = n // 2
    h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    h[:m] = a
    h[m:] = 1.0 - a[::-1]
    return h


def anti_project_vals(vals, n):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    m = n // 2
    a = 0.5 * (vals[:m] + 1.0 - vals[n - 1:m - 1:-1])
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0)


def interpolate_values(vals, n):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    n0 = len(vals)
    if n0 == n:
        return vals.copy()
    x0 = (np.arange(n0, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n0))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    return np.interp(x, x0, vals)


def upsample_first_half(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    old = len(a)
    if old == m:
        return a.copy()
    x0 = (np.arange(old, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(old)
    x = (np.arange(m, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(m)
    return np.interp(x, x0, a)


def enforce_pair_bound(a):
    """Make a_i + a_{m-1-i} <= 1, which controls the central anti-lag."""
    a = np.clip(np.asarray(a, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    b = 1.0 - a[::-1]
    return np.clip(np.minimum(a, b), 0.0, 1.0)


def start_pair_random(m, rng):
    a = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    half = m // 2
    for i in range(half):
        if rng.rand() < 0.5:
            a[i] = 1.0
        else:
            a[m - 1 - i] = 1.0
    if m % 2 == 1:
        a[m // 2] = 0.0
    return a


def make_starts(n, m, parent_values, prev_a, seed):
    starts = []
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    if parent_values is not None:
        vals = interpolate_values(parent_values, n)
        a = anti_project_vals(vals, n)
        starts.append(a)
        starts.append(enforce_pair_bound(a))

    if prev_a is not None:
        a = upsample_first_half(prev_a, m)
        starts.append(np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0))
        starts.append(enforce_pair_bound(a))

    for _ in range(3):
        starts.append(start_pair_random(m, rng))

    alphas = [0.6180339887, 0.7071067811, 0.8090169943, 0.3819660112]
    idx = np.arange(m, dtype=float)
    for alpha in alphas:
        seq = (np.mod(idx * alpha, 1.0) < 0.5).astype(float)
        starts.append(seq)
        starts.append(enforce_pair_bound(seq))

    freqs = [0.57, 1.43, 1.79, 2.37]
    for f in freqs:
        phase = 0.57 + 0.31 * rng.rand()
        seq = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * idx / float(m) + phase)
        seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1.0)
        starts.append(seq)
        starts.append(enforce_pair_bound(seq))

    for _ in range(2):
        seq = rng.rand(m).astype(float)
        ker = np.ones(5, dtype=float) / 5.0
        seq = np.convolve(seq, ker, mode="same")
        seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1.0)
        starts.append(seq)
        starts.append(enforce_pair_bound(seq))

    for i in range(len(starts)):
        starts[i] = np.clip(starts[i], 0.0, 1.0)
    return starts


def make_pre(n):
    idx = np.arange(1, n, dtype=int)
    idx_neg = (n - 1) - idx
    idx_pos = (n - 1) + idx
    return (n, idx_neg, idx_pos)


def deriv_one_into(out, h, p, n, pre):
    _, idx_neg, idx_pos = pre
    out.fill(0.0)
    center = n - 1
    out[center] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h[p]

    left = p
    if left > 0:
        arr = h[p - left:p][::-1]
        out[idx_neg[:left]] += 1.0 - arr
        out[idx_pos[:left]] -= arr

    right = n - 1 - p
    if right > 0:
        arr = h[p + 1:p + 1 + right]
        out[idx_neg[:right]] -= arr
        out[idx_pos[:right]] += 1.0 - arr


def coord_f_val(c, g, buf, center, low, high, t):
    buf[:] = g
    buf *= t
    buf += c
    t2 = t * t
    buf[center] = c[center] + g[center] * t - 2.0 * t2
    buf[low] = c[low] + g[low] * t + t2
    buf[high] = c[high] + g[high] * t + t2
    return float(buf.max())


def find_best_coord_t(c, g, buf, center, low, high, lo, hi, cur_max, g1, g2):
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
        return 0.0, cur_max

    best_t = 0.0
    best_val = cur_max

    if g1 < 2:
        g1 = 2
    if g2 < 2:
        g2 = 2

    step = (hi - lo) / float(g1 - 1)
    for j in range(g1):
        t = lo + j * step
        if abs(t) < 1e-14:
            val = cur_max
        else:
            val = coord_f_val(c, g, buf, center, low, high, t)
        if val < best_val - 1e-14:
            best_val = val
            best_t = t

    if best_val >= cur_max - 1e-11:
        return 0.0, cur_max

    jbest = int(round((best_t - lo) / step))
    if jbest < 0:
        jbest = 0
    if jbest > g1 - 1:
        jbest = g1 - 1

    left = lo + max(0, jbest - 1) * step
    right = lo + min(g1 - 1, jbest + 1) * step

    if right > left + 1e-14:
        step2 = (right - left) / float(g2 - 1)
        for j in range(g2):
            t = left + j * step2
            if abs(t) < 1e-14:
                val = cur_max
            else:
                val = coord_f_val(c, g, buf, center, low, high, t)
            if val < best_val - 1e-14:
                best_val = val
                best_t = t

    if best_val >= cur_max - 1e-12:
        return 0.0, cur_max
    return best_t, best_val


def choose_grid(n, micro):
    if micro:
        if n <= 1200:
            return 25, 21
        if n <= 2500:
            return 21, 17
        return 17, 13
    if n <= 1200:
        return 17, 15
    if n <= 2500:
        return 15, 12
    return 13, 10


def coordinate_descent_anti(a, n, deadline, seed, pre, max_move=None,
                            g1=15, g2=13):
    a = np.clip(np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        h = build_full_anti(a, n)
        c = fft_corr_count(h, n)
        return a, c5_from_counts(c, n)

    h = build_full_anti(a, n)
    c = fft_corr_count(h, n)
    cur = float(c.max())
    best_c = cur
    best_a = a.copy()

    L = 2 * n - 1
    center = n - 1

    gp = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    gq = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    fbuf = np.empty(L, dtype=float)

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    stalled = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        order = rng.permutation(m)
        significant = 0

        for ii in order:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break

            x = float(a[ii])
            p = ii
            q = n - 1 - ii
            d = q - p
            low = center - d
            high = center + d

            lo = -x
            hi = 1.0 - x
            if max_move is not None:
                if lo < -max_move:
                    lo = -max_move
                if hi > max_move:
                    hi = max_move
            if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
                continue

            deriv_one_into(gp, h, p, n, pre)
            deriv_one_into(gq, h, q, n, pre)
            gp -= gq

            t, val = find_best_coord_t(c, gp, fbuf, center, low, high,
                                        lo, hi, cur, g1, g2)
            if val < cur - 1e-11 and abs(t) > 1e-14:
                x_new = x + t
                if x_new < 0.0:
                    x_new = 0.0
                    t = x_new - x
                elif x_new > 1.0:
                    x_new = 1.0
                    t = x_new - x

                if abs(t) > 1e-14:
                    a[ii] = x_new
                    h[ii] = x_new
                    h[q] = 1.0 - x_new

                    c += gp * t
                    t2 = t * t
                    c[center] -= 2.0 * t2
                    c[low] += t2
                    c[high] += t2
                    cur = val
                    if abs(t) > 1e-6:
                        significant += 1

        if significant == 0:
            break

        h = build_full_anti(a, n)
        c = fft_corr_count(h, n)
        cur = float(c.max())

        if cur < best_c - 1e-9:
            best_c = cur
            best_a = a.copy()
            stalled = 0
        else:
            if cur > best_c + 1e-7:
                a = best_a.copy()
                h = build_full_anti(a, n)
                c = fft_corr_count(h, n)
                cur = best_c
            stalled += 1
            if stalled >= 2:
                break

    h_best = build_full_anti(best_a, n)
    c_best = fft_corr_count(h_best, n)
    c5 = c5_from_counts(c_best, n)
    return best_a, c5


def score_start(n, a):
    a = np.clip(np.asarray(a, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    h = build_full_anti(a, n)
    c = fft_corr_count(h, n)
    return c5_from_counts(c, n)


def optimize_resolution(n, phase_end, parent_values, prev_a, seed):
    m = n // 2
    starts = make_starts(n, m, parent_values, prev_a, seed)

    scored = []
    for i, a in enumerate(starts):
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0)
        c5 = score_start(n, a)
        scored.append((c5, i, a))
    scored.sort()

    if not scored:
        a0 = start_pair_random(m, np.random.RandomState(seed))
        return a0, score_start(n, a0), build_full_anti(a0, n)

    if time.time() >= phase_end - 10:
        best_a = np.clip(scored[0][2], 0.0, 1.0)
        return best_a, scored[0][0], build_full_anti(best_a, n)

    remaining = phase_end - time.time()
    k = 1
    if remaining > 70 and len(scored) >= 2:
        k = 2
    if remaining > 180 and len(scored) >= 3:
        k = 3

    if k == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif k == 2:
        fracs = [0.68, 0.32]
    else:
        fracs = [0.56, 0.29, 0.15]

    pre = make_pre(n)
    g1_full, g2_full = choose_grid(n, False)
    g1_micro, g2_micro = choose_grid(n, True)

    best_a = np.clip(scored[0][2], 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    best_c5 = scored[0][0]

    for rank in range(k):
        t_start = time.time()
        alloc = fracs[rank] * (phase_end - t_start)
        if alloc <= 25:
            break
        sub_deadline = t_start + alloc

        a0 = np.clip(scored[rank][2], 0.0, 1.0).copy()
        c0 = score_start(n, a0)
        if c0 < best_c5:
            best_c5 = c0
            best_a = a0.copy()

        full_end = min(sub_deadline, time.time() + 0.70 * alloc)
        if full_end > time.time() + 10:
            a_full, c_full = coordinate_descent_anti(
                a0, n, full_end, seed + 101 + rank, pre,
                max_move=None, g1=g1_full, g2=g2_full)
            if c_full < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c_full
                best_a = a_full.copy()
        else:
            a_full = a0

        if time.time() < sub_deadline - 8:
            micro_end = min(sub_deadline, time.time() + 0.25 * alloc)
            a_m1, c_m1 = coordinate_descent_anti(
                a_full, n, micro_end, seed + 202 + rank, pre,
                max_move=0.08, g1=g1_micro, g2=g2_micro)
            if c_m1 < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c_m1
                best_a = a_m1.copy()
        else:
            a_m1 = a_full

        if time.time() < sub_deadline - 4:
            a_m2, c_m2 = coordinate_descent_anti(
                a_m1, n, sub_deadline, seed + 303 + rank, pre,
                max_move=0.02, g1=g1_micro, g2=g2_micro)
            if c_m2 < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c_m2
                best_a = a_m2.copy()

    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1.0)
    return best_a, best_c5, build_full_anti(best_a, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 10.0

    parent_values = None
    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    try:
        raw = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        n_raw = len(raw)
        if n_raw > 0:
            h_raw = project_feasible(raw, n_raw)
            c_raw = compute_c5_exact(h_raw, n_raw)
            parent_values = h_raw
            best_h = h_raw
            best_c = c_raw
            best_n = n_raw
    except Exception:
        parent_values = None

    base_total = max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0)

    phase_specs = []
    if base_total >= 90:
        phase_specs.append((500, 0.08))
    if base_total >= 220:
        phase_specs.append((1000, 0.17))
    if base_total >= 420:
        phase_specs.append((2000, 0.34))
    if base_total >= 650:
        phase_specs.append((4000, 0.41))

    if not phase_specs:
        phase_specs.append((500, 0.95))

    prev_a = None

    for idx, (n, frac) in enumerate(phase_specs):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break

        if idx == len(phase_specs) - 1:
            phase_end = deadline
        else:
            phase_end = min(deadline, time.time() + frac * base_total)
            if phase_end - time.time() < 30:
                continue

        if phase_end - time.time() < 25:
            continue

        try:
            a_try, c_try, h_try = optimize_resolution(
                n, phase_end, parent_values, prev_a, seed + 17 * idx + 1001)
        except Exception:
            continue

        if a_try is None:
            continue

        prev_a = a_try

        if c_try < best_c + 1e-7:
            try:
                h_ev = project_feasible(h_try, n)
                c_ev = compute_c5_exact(h_ev, n)
                if c_ev < best_c:
                    best_h = h_ev
                    best_c = c_ev
                    best_n = n
            except Exception:
                pass

    if best_h is None:
        n_fb = 500
        m_fb = n_fb // 2
        a_fb = start_pair_random(m_fb, np.random.RandomState(seed))
        h_fb = project_feasible(build_full_anti(a_fb, n_fb), n_fb)
        best_h = h_fb
        best_c = compute_c5_exact(h_fb, n_fb)
        best_n = n_fb

    else:
        try:
            best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
            best_c = compute_c5_exact(best_h, best_n)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
